AUX SEND mode [SEND] (Not available when the AUX/BUS window is active.)
Knobs 1 - 8 (tweaking)             Edits the AUX Send Level.
[SHIFT] + Knobs 1 - 8 (pressing)   Resets the edited parameter to the default value (-∞).
EFF SEND mode [EFFECT] (Not available when the AUX/BUS window is active.)
Knobs 1 - 8 (tweaking)             Edits the Effect Send Level.
[SHIFT] + Knobs 1 - 8 (pressing)   Resets the edited parameter to the default value (-∞).

About MIDI                                                                                    MIDI Data Format
MIDI is an acronym that stands for Musical Instrument Digital Interface, which allows           Many MIDI messages listed in the MIDI Data Format section are expressed in hexadecimal or
electronic musical instruments to “communicate” with each other, by sending and                 binary numbers. Hexadecimal numbers may include the letter “H” as a suffix. The letter “n”
receiving compatible Note, Control Change, Program Change and various other types               indicates a certain whole number.
of MIDI data, or messages.                                                                      The chart below lists the corresponding decimal number for each hexadecimal/binary
The MOTIF ES6/7/8 can control a MIDI device by transmitting note related data and               number.
various types of controller data. The MOTIF ES6/7/8 can be controlled by the incoming
MIDI messages which automatically select MIDI channels, voices and effects, change                   Decimal Hexadecimal       Binary           Decimal Hexadecimal      Binary
parameter values, and of course play the voices specified for the various Parts in a                   0          00         0000 0000           64         40         0100 0000
Performance.                                                                                           1          01         0000 0001           65         41         0100 0001
2          02         0000 0010           66         42         0100 0010
3          03         0000 0011           67         43         0100 0011
4          04         0000 0100           68         44         0100 0100
MIDI Messages Transmitted/Received by the MOTIF                                                        5          05         0000 0101           69         45         0100 0101
6          06         0000 0110           70         46         0100 0110
ES6/7/8                                                                                                7          07         0000 0111           71         47         0100 0111
The MIDI messages can be divided into two groups: the Channel Messages and System                      8          08         0000 1000           72         48         0100 1000
Messages. Below is a brief explanation of the various types of MIDI messages which the                 9          09         0000 1001           73         49         0100 1001
MOTIF ES6/7/8 can receive/transmit.                                                                    10         0A         0000 1010           74         4A         0100 1010
11         0B         0000 1011           75         4B         0100 1011
12         0C         0000 1100           76         4C         0100 1100
13         0D         0000 1101           77         4D         0100 1101
CHANNEL MESSAGES                                                                                       14         0E         0000 1110           78         4E         0100 1110
15         0F         0000 1111           79         4F         0100 1111
The Channel Messages are the data related to the performance on the keyboard for the                   16         10         0001 0000           80         50         0101 0000
specific channel.                                                                                      17         11         0001 0001           81         51         0101 0001
18         12         0001 0010           82         52         0101 0010
19         13         0001 0011           83         53         0101 0011
• Note On/Note Off (Key On/Key Off)
20         14         0001 0100           84         54         0101 0100
Messages which are generated when the keyboard is played. Each message includes a
21         15         0001 0101           85         55         0101 0101
specific note number which corresponds to the key which is pressed, plus a velocity                          16         0001 0110           86         56         0101 0110
value based on how hard the key is struck.                                                        23         17         0001 0111           87         57         0101 0111
• Control Change                                                                                    24         18         0001 1000           88         58         0101 1000
25         19         0001 1001           89         59         0101 1001
Control Change messages let you select a voice bank (Bank Select), control volume,
26         1A         0001 1010           90         5A         0101 1010
panning, modulation, portamento time, brightness and various other controller
27         1B         0001 1011           91         5B         0101 1011
parameters, through specific Control Change numbers which correspond to each of the               28         1C         0001 1100           92         5C         0101 1100
various parameters.                                                                               29         1D         0001 1101           93         5D         0101 1101
30         1E         0001 1110           94         5E         0101 1110
• Program Change
31         1F         0001 1111           95         5F         0101 1111
Messages which voice to select for each Part. With a combination of Bank Select, you
32         20         0010 0000           96         60         0110 0000
can select not only basic voice numbers, but also variation voice bank numbers.                   33         21         0010 0001           97         61         0110 0001
• Channel Aftertouch                                                                                34         22         0010 0010           98         62         0110 0010
35         23         0010 0011           99         63         0110 0011
Messages which let you control the sounds by the pressure you apply to the keys after
36         24         0010 0100           100        64         0110 0100
the initial striking of the keys, over the entire channel.
37         25         0010 0101           101        65         0110 0101
• Polyphonic Key Pressure                                                                           38         26         0010 0110           102        66         0110 0110
Messages which let you control the sounds by the pressure you apply to the keys after             39         27         0010 0111           103        67         0110 0111
the initial striking of the keys, for each individual key. The internal sequencer (MOTIF          40         28         0010 1000           104        68         0110 1000
41         29         0010 1001           105        69         0110 1001
ES6/7/8) can send this message.
42         2A         0010 1010           106        6A         0110 1010
• Pitch Bend                                                                                        43         2B         0010 1011           107        6B         0110 1011
Pitch Bend messages are continuous controller messages that allow the pitch of                    44         2C         0010 1100           108        6C         0110 1100
designated notes to be raised or lowered by a specified amount over a specified                   45         2D         0010 1101           109        6D         0110 1101
46         2E         0010 1110           110        6E         0110 1110
duration.
47         2F         0010 1111           111        6F         0110 1111
48         30         0011 0000           112        70         0111 0000
49         31         0011 0001           113        71         0111 0001
50         32         0011 0010           114        72         0111 0010
SYSTEM MESSAGES
51         33         0011 0011           115        73         0111 0011
The System Messages are the data related to the overall system of the device.                          52         34         0011 0100           116        74         0111 0100
53         35         0011 0101           117        75         0111 0101
• Exclusive Messages                                                                                54         36         0011 0110           118        76         0111 0110
Exclusive messages control various functions of the MOTIF ES6/7/8, including bulk                 55         37         0011 0111           119        77         0111 0111
dump, master volume and master tuning, effect type and various other parameters.                  56         38         0011 1000           120        78         0111 1000
57         39         0011 1001           121        79         0111 1001
• Realtime Messages                                                                                 58         3A         0011 1010           122        7A         0111 1010
Messages which control the sequencer including Start, Stop, Continue, MIDI clock and              59         3B         0011 1011           123        7B         0111 1011
Active Sensing messages.                                                                          60         3C         0011 1100           124        7C         0111 1100
61         3D         0011 1101           125        7D         0111 1101
• Common Messages                                                                                   62         3E         0011 1110           126        7E         0111 1110
Messages which also control the sequencer including Song Select and Song Position                 63         3F         0011 1111           127        7F         0111 1111
Pointer messages.
Additional Notes
• For example, 144 - 159(Decimal)/9nH/1001 0000 - 1001 1111(Binary) indicate the note-on
messages for the channels 1 through 16 respectively. 176 - 191/BnH/1011 0000 - 1011
1111 indicate the control change messages for the channels 1 through 16 respectively.
192 -207/CnH/1100 0000 - 1100 1111 indicate the program change messages for the
channels 1 through 16 respectively. 240/F0H/1111 0000 is positioned at the beginning of
data to indicate a system exclusive message. 247/F7H/1111 0111 is positioned at the end
of the system exclusive message.

• aaH (Hexadecimal)/0aaaaaaa (Binary) indicates the data addresses. The data address
consists of High, Mid and Low.

• bbH/0bbbbbbb indicates byte counts.

• ccH/0ccccccc indicates check sums.

• ddH/0ddddddd indicates data/value.

                  send MMC(stop,deffered play,locate) if sync mode is MTC.
                  receive MTC quarter frame message if sync mode is MTC.
